## Response:
23/03/2026, 11:51:03

At **5'5" and 47–49 kg**, you’re pretty underweight for a muscular look. ## What your goal should be

- **Short-term goal:** **54–56 kg**
- **Great aesthetic healthy goal:** **58–62 kg**

Even gaining **5–7 kg properly** will make you look very different.

You need 3 things:

### 1. Strength training
Start with **3 days a week** only. Best beginner split:
- **Day 1: Push** — chest, shoulders, triceps
- **Day 2: Pull** — back, biceps
- **Day 3: Legs + core**

### Day 1 – Push
- Machine chest press or dumbbell bench press – 3 sets
- Incline dumbbell press – 3 sets
- Shoulder press – 3 sets
- Lateral raises – 3 sets
- Tricep pushdowns – 3 sets
- Overhead tricep extension – 2 sets

### Day 2 – Pull
- Lat pulldown – 3 sets
- Seated cable row – 3 sets
- One-arm dumbbell row – 3 sets
- Face pulls – 2 sets
- Dumbbell bicep curls – 3 sets
- Hammer curls – 2 sets

### Day 3 – Legs + core
- Leg press or goblet squat – 3 sets
- Romanian deadlift with dumbbells – 3 sets
- Leg curl – 3 sets
- Calf raises – 3 sets
- Plank – 3 rounds
- Hanging knee raises or crunches – 3 sets

### Reps
For almost everything:
- **8–12 reps**
- Use a weight where last 2 reps feel hard, but form stays good

## Your real goal in gym for first 3 months
Not ego lifting. Your goals:
- learn proper form
- become consistent
- slowly increase weights
- gain 0.25 to 0.5 kg per week
- eat enough daily

## 2. You must eat more
This is the main part. Skinny beginners usually fail here, not in gym.

### Protein goal
Aim for **75–95 grams protein per day**

### Simple eating rule
Eat:
- **3 proper meals**
- **2 extra snacks/shakes**

### Example simple muscle gain add-ons
Add 1 or 2 of these daily:
- 2 bananas + milk + peanut butter shake
- 4 eggs
- paneer sandwich
- curd rice + extra paneer
- peanut butter bread
- handful of nuts and raisins

## 3. Sleep
Super important.
Aim for **7.5–9 hours**.

### In 1 month
- body won’t transform suddenly
- strength improves
- appetite improves
- slight fullness starts

### In 3 months
- arms look better
- shoulders get some shape
- chest improves
- face may look healthier
- weight can go up by 2–4 kg if eating properly

### In 6–12 months
You can genuinely look like:
- “healthy gym guy”
- not bulky
- noticeable arms and upper body
- much better posture and confidence

## Biggest mistakes you should avoid
- doing too much cardio
- skipping legs
- changing workout every week
- not eating enough
- thinking protein alone is enough
- going 6 days a week and quitting
- comparing yourself to guys with 3+ years gym experience
- trying to stay “lean” while already underweight

### First 3 months
- go gym **3 times/week**
- gain **2–4 kg**
- hit protein goal most days
- learn form on basic exercises

### By 6 months
- reach around **52–55 kg**
- visibly better arms/shoulders
- feel stronger
- clothes fit better

### By 12 months
- aim **56–60 kg**
- maintain decent body fat
- look athletic, not bulky

## Supplements?
Not necessary, but useful:
- **Whey protein** if you can’t hit protein from food
- **Creatine monohydrate 3–5g/day** can help strength and muscle gain
- No need for mass gainers unless eating is impossible

## Super simple starter version
Do this for next 4 weeks:
- gym **Mon/Wed/Fri**
- 45–60 min each
- eat 1 extra shake daily
- eat protein in every meal
- track bodyweight every morning
- try to gain around **1–2 kg in the month**

Roughly, that shake is probably in the **450 to 700 calorie** range, depending mostly on **how much milk** and **how much peanut butter** you use.

- **1 banana**: ~90–110 kcal  
- **3–4 small dates**: ~60–100 kcal  
- **5–6 almonds**: ~35–45 kcal  
- **5–6 cashews**: ~45–55 kcal  
- **Bournvita (1–2 tbsp)**: ~35–80 kcal  
- **Milk**
  - **250 ml**: ~150 kcal if full-fat, ~90–110 if toned
  - **400 ml**: ~240 kcal if full-fat, ~140–180 if toned
- **Peanut butter chocolate flavoured**
  - **1 tbsp**: ~90–100 kcal
  - **2 tbsp**: ~180–200 kcal

So examples:

### If it’s a lighter version
- 250 ml toned milk
- 1 tbsp peanut butter
- 1 banana
- dates + a few nuts
- little Bournvita

That’s around **450–520 kcal**

### If it’s a stronger bulk shake
- 400 ml full-fat milk
- 2 tbsp peanut butter
- 1 banana
- 4 dates
- nuts
- 1–2 tbsp Bournvita

That’s around **600–750 kcal**

A decent estimate for protein:
- milk: ~8–13g depending on amount
- peanut butter: ~4–8g
- nuts: ~2–4g
- banana/dates/Bournvita: very little

So total maybe **15–25g protein**, depending on quantity.

Good — this is actually the **most important beginner question**.  
If you get this right, your progress will be smooth.

Forget numbers like “5kg, 10kg.”  
Use this rule instead:

## ✅ The PERFECT weight =  
You can do **8–12 reps**, and the **last 2 reps feel hard**, but:
- form is still correct  
- you’re not shaking like crazy  
- you don’t fail completely

- If you can do **15+ reps easily** → ❌ too light  
- If you struggle at **5–6 reps** → ❌ too heavy  
- If you reach **8–12 reps with effort** → ✅ perfect

# 💪 For YOU (first 2 weeks)

## Step 1: First set = trial set
Pick a light-medium weight and test.

## Step 2: Adjust
- too easy → increase  
- too hard → decrease

## Step 3: Lock that weight for that exercise

### Dumbbell Curl
- Try 5 kg:
  - if you do 12 reps easily → go 7.5 kg  
- Try 7.5 kg:
  - if you reach 10 reps and last reps are hard → ✅ perfect

# 🏋️ Machine exercises (important)

Still follow same rule:
👉 **8–12 reps, last reps hard**

When to increase?

## 👉 Choose weight where:

# 🟥 1. Machine Chest Press  
👉 **20–25 kg**  
👉 3 × 8–12

✔️ If hard → stay 20

# 🟥 2. Incline Dumbbell Press  
👉 **5 kg each hand** (start)  
👉 3 × 8–12

✔️ If easy → try 7.5 kg

# 🟧 3. Dumbbell Shoulder Press  
👉 **2.5–5 kg each**  
👉 3 × 8–12

✔️ Start 2.5 → go 5 if comfortable

# 🟨 4. Lateral Raises  
👉 **2 kg each**  
👉 3 × 10–12

# 🟪 5. Tricep Pushdown (Cable)  
👉 **10–15 kg**  
👉 3 × 10–12

# 🟫 6. Bench Dips  
👉 **Bodyweight**  
👉 2 × 8–12

|----------|--------|

# 🧠 What is GOATLife?

- oats + whey protein + seeds  
- gives **~20g protein per serving** ([Amazon](https://www.amazon.in/GoOAT-Overnight-Management-Breakfast-Spoonfree/dp/B0CCFBXMGR?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 💪 Why it exists

👉 basically:
## **“drinkable / easy breakfast”** ([Goat life](https://goatlife.co.in/?srsltid=AfmBOoqsnY8BeCKKJzYQ7sETQ8NeMxINps7fL5jwmk2OjzkbDyizzo2M&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 👉 Method 1 (quick)

1. open packet  
2. add milk or water  
3. shake / mix  
4. eat or drink

## 👉 Method 2 (better taste)

1. add milk  
2. keep in fridge overnight  
3. eat next morning

👉 called **overnight oats** ([Instagram](https://www.instagram.com/reel/Cw-Ua1iMPVi/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 📊 Nutrition (important)

## 👉 Per serving:
- ~20g protein ✔️  
- carbs from oats ✔️  
- fibre ✔️

# ⚖️ Should YOU use it?

## 👉 Use it when:

| | Shake | GOATLife |
|--|--|--|
| Calories | higher | moderate |
| Protein | ~20g | ~20g |
| Filling | more | medium |
